# Claims register

One row per claim this repository makes, with the analyses that stand behind
it, the artefact a reader can open, its status in the root vocabulary
(Validated, Descriptive only, Exploratory, Retracted-superseded, Not
established), and a plain judgement of whether the result has potential: a
figure or sentence that carries a hypothesis forward, a lead that needs one more
analysis, or a dead end kept for the record. A claim with no logged artefact
is Not established by definition. Status changes only on the owner's
retain/reject decision (DEVELOPMENT.md); rejected rows stay in the table.
Rows C169 onward, and the dated qualifiers added to earlier rows on
2026-09-29, were proposed under the owner's request to register every claim
and are marked as delegated reassessments until the owner reviews them.

Numbers quoted here come from the artefact named in the row. The stepwise
account of how the analyses relate to each other is
[`Research Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/ANALYSIS_TRIAL_PLAN.md`](Research%20Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/ANALYSIS_TRIAL_PLAN.md).

## Stage 0. Initial run (August 2026): recovering the published biology from raw counts

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | Blind clustering recovers the deposited mouse cell types (median purity 0.947 over 107,626 labelled cells) | per-sample QC, Scrublet per capture, no batch correction, Leiden 0.3; labels held out and graded afterwards | `Research Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/GSE262927/tables/cluster_vs_author_celltype_fraction.csv`; checked by `analysis/scripts/validate_repository.py` | Validated | Yes. The annotation-robustness credential every lab reads first; the atlas UMAP is already the README figure. |
| C3 | The injury capillary state has not resolved by 366 dpi (2.0% at baseline, 37.5% at 25 dpi, 21.7% at 366 dpi) | capillary subset reclustered within compartment; per-animal abundance by day | `Research Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/GSE262927/regeneration_focus/tables/icap_abundance_per_sample.csv` | Validated | Moderate. It carries the "persistent injury state" theme; use it as the template for the interstitial-macrophage lead (C13). |
| C4 | The injury capillary state has a CAP1 origin (Kit line traced at 33 to 53% per animal) (qualifier 2026-09-29: the reporter-detected traced fraction in iCAP exceeds that in other endothelium in each of the 3 Kit animals, 33.41 against 31.71, 42.27 against 35.01 and 52.71 against 42.30%; this descriptive enrichment is lineage-trace support for a CAP1 contribution, not proof of an exclusive CAP1 origin); the CAP2 lines are uninformative, not negative | 8-sample Cre cohort; trace rule reproduces the deposited calls at 100% | `Research Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/GSE262927/lineage_tracing_cohort/` | Descriptive only (Kit: three animals, one harvest day, enrichment over other endothelium, not origin proof); Not established (CAP2; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Moderate. A lineage-trace method credential; low direct pull for the four labs. The discriminating comparison is the iCAP traced fraction against the traced fraction of the CAP1 source population itself in each Kit animal (labelling efficiency); the other-EC denominator mixes CAP1, CAP2 and other endothelium. Lineage labelling shows descent of labelled cells, not the state transition mechanism. |

## Stage 1. Follow-ups (2026-09-10): the source paper's phase and myeloid claims

Rules frozen before each run; unit is the animal; medians; no P values (two
animals per active-repair day). Owner review pending on every row.

Both original cohorts used a gene-permutation null, so correlation-related calibration limits apply to both. Official voom/TMM plus CAMERA yields zero corrected two-cohort candidates with estimated residual correlation, but 30 with fixed correlation 0.01 (1 AT2, 1 fibroblast, 28 macrophage), of which 28 overlap the historical 62. Neither setting is selected because it gives more hits: the primary estimated-correlation specification remains primary, the fixed setting is a declared sensitivity. Directional leads remain exploratory and method-sensitive, not robustly validated or biologically absent. Subtype, ambient-RNA, floor-selection and observational-design limits remain; overlapping sets are not independent mechanisms. Prior owner retention is preserved in decision 28. |

The deposited aMAC label may include monocyte-derived cells that have not finished converting, so a large C1q fold change can be composition inside the label as well as state; one deposit, one arm of four animals A lower later mean is not absence of the programme. Deposited aMAC pseudobulks still mix state and composition; nonsignificant proliferation genes do not establish equivalence. |
| C163 | A pre-registered proliferation or arginine and ornithine gene set separates the alveolar macrophage population between repair phases | trial W1 under CAMERA on the same design: no set at FDR < 0.05 in either contrast (0.95 in late active repair against resolution, 0.46 to 0.48 in resolution against long-term); the proliferation sets carry an inter-gene correlation of 0.30 to 0.45 across animals, and the two hallmark sets a variance inflation of 55 to 69, so a hallmark set of about 190 genes carries the independent information of about three; the ornithine set had 7 genes and was not tested Official reference limma CAMERA reproduces the no-hit result under both historical logCPM and voom/TMM transformations with estimated correlation. | `Research Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/trials/w1_amac_pseudobulk_de/w1_sets.csv`; `analysis/corrections/statistics/tables/w1_reference_camera.csv` | Not established (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Moderate, for the method more than the biology: an absence at these arms bounds nothing (rule R9), but the correlation it measured is the reason a gene-permutation null, as used for C158 and discovery in C161, can report FDR 0.000 for a proliferation set. Whether C158 survives a correlation-respecting test on its own myeloid pseudobulk is untested The original custom statistic is retained as historical. The undersized seven-gene ornithine set was not tested, and failed significance is not equivalence or absence of a circuit. |
| C164 | The original W1 long-term aMAC pseudobulk contrast has higher NF-kB and immediate-early transcripts than the pooled 42/90-dpi resolution group | trial W1 contrast B, 8 against 3 animals, the arm identified inside infection round 2022-12-06: 100 genes at padj < 0.05, 71 higher at 366 dpi, led by Il1a, Tnf, Nfkbiz, Nfkbid, Ccrl2, Zfp36, S100a8 and Cd274; Mki67 alone is higher at 90 dpi (log2 fold change 1.19, padj 0.02) | `Research Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/trials/w1_amac_pseudobulk_de/w1_de_top_B.csv` | Descriptive only (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Low as injury biology: the contrast is confounded with about nine months of age (ageing alone changes alveolar macrophages, McQuattie-Pimentel et al. 2021, doi:10.1172/JCI140299), with sex inside the round, and with harvest date, and Tnf, Il1a, Nfkbiz and Zfp36 are also dissociation-stress genes (van den Brink et al. 2017, reference M8). Nothing here separates persistence after injury from age or processing |

## Stage 2. Proposed, not run

No claims. The proposals, what each needs, and its stop condition are in the
trial plan (W1 myeloid pseudobulk programme, S1 receptor and STAT
layers, Nb1 niche candidates at 11 dpi, D1 gate check on
external Treg series, V1 human validation of the myeloid states). The lead
with the most potential is C13, because it turns the repository's
persistent-state theme toward the immune compartment; the one with the
least is C16, which this series cannot settle.

## Stage 3. Cardoso 2026, the Gate 2 paper (2026-09-12)

Branch 2C (the Choi axis) of the reading order since 2026-09-15; see
[`Research Article/README.md`](Research%20Article/README.md). The trial gates named in this
section (0, 1, 2a, 2b, 2d) are the owner's analysis gates for this paper,
not reading-order gates.

A different deposit with a different ceiling. Every mouse library of this
paper pools three mice and each genotype contributes one library per sort, so
**no genotype contrast in it carries within-group replication**: no row drawn
from this paper's own deposit is tested, none carries a P value, and the
highest status such a row can reach is Descriptive only. The companion series
GSE247505 is a partial exception, with two libraries per arm and three time
points, but its unit is still the library. Rows C37 and later leave this
deposit for public datasets whose unit is the donor or the animal, which is
what makes C37 the only tested row in the section that holds. Row C49 is the
first direct test of the paper's epithelium-to-fibroblast link, and it is a
null with its detectable effect size stated. Trials and
their frozen rules: [`Research Article/gate2_05_cardoso_2026/ANALYSIS_TRIAL_PLAN.md`](Research%20Article/gate2_05_cardoso_2026/ANALYSIS_TRIAL_PLAN.md).
Owner review pending on every row.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

A depth-matched control is still owed. |

## Stage 4. Choi 2020, roadmap paper 2, re-entered at the owner's direction (2026-09-15)

The paper that defines the DATP state the Cardoso rows lean on, whose first
author leads the owner's laboratory. Its deposit check (trial D0) ran on
2026-09-13; the folder was withdrawn on 2026-09-15 until the owner had read
the paper, and re-entered the same day at the owner's direction with trials
D1 to D7 and the corrected passes D2b and D5b (DEVELOPMENT decisions 21 and
23). Rows C85 to C104 are those trials' outcomes, added 2026-09-15. The deposit has one library per
condition, **two mice pooled per library**, so no contrast in it is testable
and the highest status a between-condition row can reach is Descriptive
only; an ordering inside one library and a permutation inside one library
are the two exceptions. Trials and frozen rules:
[`Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/ANALYSIS_TRIAL_PLAN.md`](Research%20Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/ANALYSIS_TRIAL_PLAN.md).
Owner review pending on every row.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| C59 | No contrast in the Choi-2020 deposit carries within-group replication | trial D0: the frozen replicate rule applied to time point with sort held constant, sort with time point held constant, and organoid treatment; none passed | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/trials/d0_data_reality_check/d0_replication.csv` | Descriptive only | High, because it is the ceiling for every trial in that folder. The paper that defines the DATP state turns out to have the same limitation the Cardoso deposit does. |
| C60 | Six of the eight Choi-2020 matrices are raw 10x barcode whitelists rather than called cells, so cell calling is this repository's job | trial D0: column count equals the 10x v2 whitelist of 737,280 in all six lineage-traced libraries; the two organoid libraries are filtered at 2,101 and 3,066 cells | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/trials/d0_data_reality_check/d0_library_inventory.csv` | Descriptive only | High and practical: the cell count will not match the paper's unless Cell Ranger 2.0.2's caller is reproduced, which it will not be, so Gate 1 needs a frozen calling threshold. |

A reanalysis must trust the library labels for lineage, the opposite of the Cardoso deposit where the BSD marker gave an independent sort check (claim C21). |

It is the outcome the trial was designed to be able to produce, and it stops the lead cleanly rather than leaving it open. |

| C78 | The double-positive call is partly a depth call: 342 of 379 such cells in the reference library sit in the deep half, and 31 of 32 and 33 of 37 in the bleomycin libraries | trial C10, depth split at the median genes per cell | `Research Article/gate2_05_cardoso_2026/trials/c10_published_state_or_not/c10_effect_sizes.csv` | Descriptive only | Moderate. Anything built on a two-sparse-gene definition inherits this, and it is why the opposite-sign argument rather than the effect size is what carries C73. |
| C79 | Trial C10's depth rule covered a half that cannot be computed | trial C10 T5: the bleomycin shallow halves hold one and four double-positive cells; the first run treated not computable as failed and suppressed a reading the reference library supports | `Research Article/gate2_05_cardoso_2026/trials/c10_published_state_or_not/c10_run_record.json` | Refuted as written, and disclosed | High as a curation-hygiene row and the fourth of its kind, after C54, C56 and C72. All four specified a threshold and left unsaid what would make their own answer unreadable. |

High matrix-pair prevalence is an expression-score property; this analysis does not determine whether those pairs signal. |

## Stage 5. The DATP epigenetics branch of roadmap paper 2 (2026-09-20)

Roadmap paper 2 supports the epigenetic half of its Il1r1 claim with ATAC-seq
deposited as two bigwig coverage tracks, which trial D0 recorded as unusable. The
branch therefore runs on two 10x multiome deposits from the Jichao Chen
laboratory, GSE310539 (Lynch 2026) and GSE247130 (Hassan and Chen 2024), and asks
whether the AT2 identity programme is closed at the chromatin level or merely
silenced at the RNA level in the CLDN4-positive transitional state. Five of the
seven trials refused, each on a different defect, and the one reading that was
produced was withdrawn by the robustness trial that followed it.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C116 | GSE247130's deposited barcode-suffix order is inverted: suffix 1 is the control and suffix 2 the Cebpa mutant | trial M1 rule R2 and trial M4: Cebpa is higher in the suffix the GEO sample order calls the Cebpa knockout in all three files, by 14.5x (P9), 10.9x (7 weeks) and 4.5x (Sendai), and a conditional knockout cannot carry more of its own target than its control. Sox9 agrees in all three (7.1x, 2.8x, 2.5x) and Cldn4 in the infected file (4.2x) | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m4_the_corroboration_the_register_claimed/m4_ratios.csv` | Validated (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High, and it is the branch's one validated result: anyone reusing that deposit as documented has the genotypes backwards. **Its evidence was mis-stated when first registered and row C151 records that**: the Sox9 axis was never computed by the run that claimed it, and two magnitudes were wrong. The map itself is unaffected. |
| C117 | Either multiome deposit can support a tested contrast between conditions | trial M0 under the replicate rule inherited from D0: ten libraries, 104,143 cells, one library per condition, each pooling two mice before loading | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m0_deposit_reality_check/m0_replication.csv` | Not establishable with the data that exists (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as the fifth and sixth deposit in this project with that ceiling. Everything in the branch is Descriptive only as a consequence. |
| C118 | The CLDN4-positive KRT8-positive alveolar group loses the AT2 identity programme in RNA, by 12.3 to 16.7 detection points across four downsampling seeds in both injured wells (m2_seeds.csv), and by 12.6 and 17.1 at M1e's single budget (m1e_arms.csv), against a sham band of twenty random splits of reference cells, while the AT1 arm does not move in any of them | trials M1e and M2; per gene the loss is carried by at least five genes (Etv5 -0.227 and -0.239, Napsa -0.272 and -0.167, Slc34a2 -0.271 and -0.189, Abca3 -0.182 and -0.212, Lamp3 -0.160 and -0.115) | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m2_robustness_of_the_m1e_reading/m2_seeds.csv` | Descriptive only (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as the branch's substantive positive result, with one limit: the two deposits share a laboratory, the lineage tools, the injury model, the kit, the aligner and this analysis code, so agreement between them is consistency and not replication. |
| C119 | A CLDN4-positive KRT8-positive transcript call identifies an injury-induced state | trial M1c: the two neonatal P9 wells label 3.69 and 8.07 per cent against at most 5.09 per cent in any injured well, so the whole-trial negative control fired and the trial refused | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m1c_label_at_the_depth_available/m1c_labels.csv` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High, and it answers the owner's first question directly. Krt8 and Cldn4 are expressed across immature postnatal alveolar epithelium, so this marker set is shared with normal development and is not by itself damage-associated. The source paper's own argument predicts this and never states it as a limitation of the marker. |
| C120 | In the CLDN4-positive group the AT2 identity programme is silenced in RNA while its chromatin stays open | trial M1e read this in two wells; trial M2 withdrew it | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m1e_per_well_budget/m1e_arms.csv` | Retracted-superseded (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as a retraction kept on display. The reading rested on rule R9, which permits a null to be read only behind a positive control that fired, and M2 shows the control fires in five of eight seed-and-well combinations and fails leave-one-out on two of its six genes. |
| C121 | Whether the AT2 identity programme's chromatin closes in the transitional state; see C131 and C133, which give the question a direction and a bound without settling it | trials M1e, M2 and M3; three sham standard deviations, the smallest value the frozen rule would have called real, is 0.0043 to 0.0120 in detection fraction, and the effects present are 0.004 to 0.012 | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m2_robustness_of_the_m1e_reading/m2_summary.md` | Not established (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as the honest state of the question. The design works at its own floor, so it cannot separate the chromatin not moving from its being unable to see it move. |
| C122 | Where the data lean at all, they lean toward the AT2 programme staying open | trial M2 and the M2 offsets table: under the mean and trimmed offsets the AT2 arm in the infected Cebpa-mutant well moves further toward closing, reaching z = -3.23 under the mean, where it clears, and z = -2.81 under the trimmed mean | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m2_robustness_of_the_m1e_reading/m2_offsets.csv` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as the guard against the retraction being read as a hint. **Two z values this row previously quoted, -2.89 and -3.55, were never computed by any trial here**: they came from an adversarial reviewer's own recomputation and the cited table holds only median, mean and trimmed. Row C151. The direction is unaffected and is in the logged table. |
| C123 | Dropping any cell with a non-zero Scgb1a1, Scgb3a2, Foxj1 or Krt5 count removes airway cells | trial M1 rule R3: the rule removed a median of 99.9 per cent of cells across the ten wells, keeping 25.5 per cent of the shallowest well and none of the deepest | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m1_closed_or_merely_silenced/m1_wells.csv` | Refuted as written, and disclosed (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as a general lesson for single-nucleus data: presence of an abundant secreted transcript is ambient, not identity, and a presence rule built on one is a depth filter under another name. The detection rates this row previously quoted for Scgb1a1 and Scgb3a2 are not in any tracked table and are dropped; the retention figures above are. |
| C124 | A label requiring Cldn4 detection and Krt8 above the uninjured 99th percentile can recover the published transitional fraction | trial M1b: on RNA downsampled to 2,366 and 3,400 UMI the conjunction returned 0.03 to 0.66 per cent of filtered cells across the ten wells | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m1b_corrected_pass/m1b_labels.csv` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | Moderate. **This row previously called the ceiling arithmetic and it is not**: it multiplied two marginals as if independent, and the one-per-cent term is a property of the uninjured well the threshold was frozen from, not of the injured wells the rule was applied to. The bound on a conjunction is the smaller marginal. What the trial measured is a dropout ceiling on these matrices at this budget. Row C151. |
| C125 | Trial M1d's budget rule and its drop gate are jointly satisfiable | trial M1d: the budget retained 60 per cent of every well, which permits up to 40 per cent to fall below it, while the gate refused any well losing more than 20 per cent of a group. 5 of the eight mature wells reached the gate and all 5 refused there, at 27 to 47 per cent; the other 3 refused earlier at the fifty-cell floor and never reached the budget | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m1d_mature_wells_only/m1d_readings.csv` | Refuted, and disclosed (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as the eighth instance of this repository's disclosed defect shape and the second of the sub-shape the C12 scope conflict was. **This row previously said all eight wells refused at the gate; 3 of them never reached it.** Row C151. |
| C126 | Testing a positive control once is enough to license reading a null beside it | trial M2 against trial M1e's rule R9: the control clears in five of eight seed-and-well combinations and fails leave-one-out on two of six genes in each injured well. The two carriers differ between wells: Ndrg1, Sfn in the wildtype infected well and Lgals3, Ndrg1 in the Cebpa-mutant infected well | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m2_robustness_of_the_m1e_reading/m2_leave_one_out.csv` | Refuted, and disclosed (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as the ninth of its kind and the most useful. A sensitivity control has to clear under resampling and under leave-one-out before any null beside it may be read. **This row previously named Sfn and Ndrg1 as the carriers in both wells; in the Cebpa-mutant well Sfn is the smallest contributor of the six and dropping it raises the arm.** Row C151. |
| C127 | Counting peaks whose detection fraction differs by more than 0.10 between RNA-labelled groups measures chromatin rather than depth | refused a priori, and the argument is what carries it: the labels and the chromatin come from one nucleus, both modalities share that nucleus's quality, and a per-peak detection fraction is monotone in depth, so the null is false by construction and the design has no state in which it reads no | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/ANALYSIS_TRIAL_PLAN.md` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High. **The three numbers this row previously cited as what settled it (3,393 peaks, a permuted null mean of 2,617 and p = 0/200) were produced by an adversarial agent and never by any trial in this repository**: there is no script, no table, no run record and no seed, so they are Not established by this repository's own rule and are withdrawn from the row. The refutation stands on the argument, which needs no measurement. Row C151. |
| C128 | Anything about the AP-1 dependence of the RNA or chromatin results | trials M1e and M2: the infected Fos/Fosb/Junb mutant well held 44 labelled cells after the fragment budget, below the floor of 50, so it was never computed | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m1e_per_well_budget/m1e_readings.csv` | Not established (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | Moderate. The one well that could have spoken to the branch's most interesting genotype is the one that fell short, by six cells. |

| C129 | Trial M2's reading rested on one instrument used consistently | trial M3: rule R12 had removed Cebpa from the AT2 arm where it is genetically deleted and kept it everywhere else, and Cebpa carries three distal peaks but the largest positive per-gene value in wildtype_SeV. With Cebpa dropped everywhere the AT2 arm is negative in all three computable wells | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m3_one_instrument_and_the_right_null/m3_arms.csv` | Refuted, and disclosed (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High. The two deposits were never comparable, and the one discordant gene was the largest contributor to the point estimate in the deposit that kept it. Found by adversarial review, not by this repository. |
| C130 | A cell-permutation sham band is a sufficient null for a gene-set arm | trial M3: the sham permutes cells, so it asks whether this split is special and cannot ask whether these genes are special. Against 300 gene sets matched gene by gene on distal peak count and evaluated on the real split, the mean is +0.00069 and +0.00205 rather than zero | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m3_one_instrument_and_the_right_null/m3_arms.csv` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High as a method result that generalises past this branch. It also explains why the genome-wide median offset came out exactly 0.0: a fifth to a third of arm peaks are detected in no cell of either group. |
| C131 | The M3 AT2 distal-accessibility contrast is below its matched-gene-set null in three evaluable wells, including uninjured 7-week Cebpa mutant | trial M3 under one instrument and the gene-set null: percentile 0.000, 0.007 and 0.000 of 300 matched sets, at -37.8, -18.3 and -15.5 per cent of reference accessibility | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m3_one_instrument_and_the_right_null/m3_arms.csv` | Exploratory (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Exploratory conditional accessibility contrast. The -37.8%, -18.3%, -15.5% readings subtract a matched-set null mean before dividing by reference accessibility; raw contrasts are -31.8%, -16.0%, -8.5%. The uninjured comparator is 7-week Cebpa mutant, so its response can be genuine genetic plasticity. No well passes all original gates. Reference-cell resampling does not estimate animal-level uncertainty, and accessibility loss does not establish arrest or reversibility. Prior owner retention is preserved in decision 30. |
| C132 | Most of what M1e counted as transitional-arm signal in the infected Cebpa-mutant well was chromatin rather than the uncorrected global shift | trial M3: re-centred on the gene-set null that arm falls from clearing to z = 1.96, and only wildtype_SeV retains a transitional signal after proper centring | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m3_one_instrument_and_the_right_null/m3_arms.csv` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High, and it is the reason rule R9 now refuses the better-powered well. The offset was absent rather than inert, exactly as the review said. |
| C133 | A biological-sample-level reduction in AT2 distal accessibility is established in the infected Cebpa-mutant transitional group | trial M3, reported but refused under rule R9 because that well's positive control did not fire | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m3_one_instrument_and_the_right_null/m3_readings.csv` | Not established (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Not established at the biological-sample level. The -15.5% is a background-centered contrast relative to reference accessibility, not remaining accessibility. The -24.4 to -6.6 interval is calibrated by reference-only cell splits; it is neither an animal-level confidence interval nor all-source uncertainty for the effect. Prior owner retention is preserved in decision 30. |

## Stage 6. The Axin2 and Il1r1 assessment, a branch of roadmap paper 2 (2026-09-20)

Choi 2020 closes its Discussion by proposing a comparison of Il1r1-positive and
Axin2-positive AT2 cells. The owner raised the same question independently. This
branch assessed it and deliberately ran no trial on it, because the definitive
version cannot be run on any data that exists. One feasibility measurement was
made, to decide whether a chromatin route escapes the floor that kills the
transcript route.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C134 | Axin2-positive AT2 cells are a distinct subset of AT2 cells | the Axin2 fraction differs 20 to 30 fold between two knock-in alleles (1 per cent in Nabhan 2018, about 20 per cent in Zacharias 2018) and nobody has explained the gap; the stability argument cannot separate subset from state because a recombined cell stays labelled after it switches the gene off; and the same paper reports 73 per cent of AT2 turning Axin2 on after injury, marker status tracking distance to a Wnt5a-expressing fibroblast, and Wnt ligand switching it on in ordinary AT2 cells | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Not established (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High as a correction to a premise this project was about to build on. Choi 2020 states the subset claim as settled when citing it, and the Morrisey laboratory's own developmental data (Frank 2016) concluded cells enter and leave the state |
| C135 | Il1r1-positive AT2 cells are a distinct subset rather than an injury-inducible state | the lineage-labelled fraction rises from 15 to 60 per cent after bleomycin, and Choi's own Limitations section says residual tamoxifen means expansion of a pre-existing pool cannot be separated from fresh Cre firing during repair | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Not established (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High, and it is the paper's own disclosure rather than an attack on it. The washout longer than 16 days that would settle it has not been run |
| C136 | Axin2-positive and Il1r1-positive AT2 cells overlap | England et al. 2025 report Axin2 specifically upregulated in the Il1r1-lineage sorted fraction and write that this suggests enrichment; it is one supplementary panel, bulk-sorted, at homeostasis only, with no contingency table | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Not established (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Bulk Axin2 enrichment in an Il1r1-lineage sorted population is a descriptive association. It does not quantify same-cell current Axin2/Il1r1 overlap or joint responsiveness; lineage history and current transcript are different measurements. No assertion that this is the only evidence in the field is retained without a dated search. Prior block retention remains in decision 30. |
| C137 | No public dataset carries an Axin2 or Wnt readout and an Il1r1 readout in the same cells | the GEO DataSets keyword intersection of the two genes returns zero records across all organisms, and no Il1r1 reporter mouse has ever been deposited | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Refuted by this branch's own artefacts (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High, and it is a self-refutation worth keeping on display. The intersection searches deposit METADATA TEXT and cannot answer whether two genes are measured in the same cells; every droplet transcriptome measures both. This branch's own `locus_vs_transcript.csv` and `module_detection.csv` carry Axin2 and Il1r1 readouts in the same cells, and rows C139 and C147 depend on that co-measurement. What is true, and what the row should have said, is that **no deposit carries the two REPORTER-DEFINED populations in the same cells**, which is a different and much narrower statement. Row C151. |
| C138 | Axin2 or Il1r1 transcript detection can stand in for the lineage reporters in droplet single-cell data | measured on Choi's own deposit GSE145031: Axin2 detected in 4.6, 4.3 and 5.6 per cent of AT2-lineage cells and Il1r1 in 5.8, 7.1 and 7.1 per cent, both at about one UMI per positive cell. Nabhan's own supplement also concedes the reporter does not label all Axin2-positive cells | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Not established (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Sparse current transcripts are not validated substitutes for historical lineage or activity reporters. The quoted 4–7% range belongs to GSE145031; GSE310539 Il1r1 detection is 19.3–29.8% in the RQ figure table. No paired reporter/transcript gold-standard comparison here estimates sensitivity or specificity, so general substitution is unestablished rather than quantitatively disproved. Prior block retention remains in decision 30. |
| C139 | At least one linked Axin2 peak is detected more often than its transcript in the tested multiome wells | feasibility measurement on GSE310539 and GSE247130: at least one of Axin2's 15 to 16 linked peaks is detected in 22.1 and 39.4 per cent of cells against a transcript detected in 5.3 and 3.8 per cent, a gain of 4.1 and 10.4 fold. Three controls order correctly in both deposits: Sftpc (universal transcript, 2 peaks) is worse, Etv5 is even, Lgr5 shows the largest gain | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/feasibility_of_the_joint_question/locus_vs_transcript.csv` | Descriptive only (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | The union of several linked peaks increases detection relative to one transcript. This is an assay-feasibility observation, not evidence that locus accessibility identifies current Wnt activity, improves biological specificity or solves the same-cell responsiveness question. Prior block retention remains in decision 30. |
| C140 | An accessible Axin2 locus identifies a Wnt-responsive cell | not measured here and not claimed; accessibility reports that a locus permits expression, not that the cell is signalling, and it is slower and more permissive than transcription | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Not established (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High as the caveat that must appear in the pre-registration of any trial built on row C139, rather than in a footnote afterwards |
| C141 | The laboratory that proposed the comparison has not advanced it since 2020 | examined the 2026 Seminars in Immunology review of Yu, Lee, Choi and Choi, whose 162-item reference list cites Choi 2020 and contains no primary Axin2 or AEP paper | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High, and the error is this branch's own. **The laboratory that proposed the comparison is Joo-Hyeon Lee's, the senior author of Choi 2020, and that laboratory DID advance it**, in England et al. 2025, which row C136 cites two rows earlier as the only direct evidence in the field. The review examined belongs to a different, later laboratory. The observation about that review's bibliography may still be true and is no longer evidence for the claim it was attached to. Row C151. |

| C142 | The Axin2 and Il1r1 loci are more or less open together in the same nuclei than comparable pairs of loci are | trials A1, A1b and A1c on the eight mature wells of both multiome deposits, with three instruments; every well returned NOT COMPUTABLE because the positive control gating the reading never cleared | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/ROUTE_B_OUTCOME.md` | Not established (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High as the outcome of the one route the assessment opened. The closure is a decision under a rule frozen before the trial ran, not a demonstration that the approach cannot work, and the outcome page states what a correct gate would be |
| C143 | Krt8 and Krt18, 24 kilobases apart, share enough linked peaks to serve as an arithmetic positive control for locus co-accessibility | trial A1: the vendor annotation assigns each peak to its NEAREST gene, so the two loci largely do not share peaks; the control returned ratios of 1.11 to 1.25 against null means near 1.03, a real but modest effect rather than an identity | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/a1_locus_co_accessibility/a1_pairs.csv` | Refuted, and disclosed (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | Moderate as a trap for anyone building a positive control out of genomic proximity: nearest-gene annotation splits the intergenic peaks rather than sharing them |
| C144 | A split-locus control, two halves of one gene's peak set, cannot fail for biological reasons | trials A1b and A1c: it failed in every well at z +0.24 to +2.05, for two reasons found only afterwards. It is judged against a null built for a different peak-count profile, 15 against 38, while the control is 15 against 15; and peaks the vendor annotation links to one gene need not lie in one regulatory domain | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/a1c_graded_statistic/a1c_pairs.csv` | Refuted, and disclosed (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High as the tenth instance of this repository's disclosed defect shape, and the second time in two days that a positive control has been the thing that broke. A control asserted to be an identity must be shown to be one |
| C145 | The graded locus statistic passes two recorded positive-control comparisons that the binary version did not | trial A1c against A1b: the binary form detected nothing anywhere, while the graded form clears on Etv5 against Abca3 at z = +3.55 and on Krt8 against Krt18 at z = +3.59 | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/a1c_graded_statistic/a1c_pairs.csv` | Descriptive only (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | This is a within-trial method comparison. Passing selected positive controls does not establish calibrated sensitivity to all biological co-accessibility or rescue failed controls in the target wells. Prior block retention remains in decision 30. |

| C146 | Bulk Wnt-sorted GSE150957 measurements can directly resolve current Axin2/Il1r1 same-cell co-occurrence | this folder proposed it as Route C and the owner pointed out it is out of focus. Bulk cannot answer a co-occurrence question at any level of replication, because the question is whether two markers sit in the same cell and a sorted fraction reports an average; it also sorts on a Wnt activity reporter rather than Axin2, is whole distal epithelium rather than AT2, and uses a fifth injury model | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/README.md` | Refuted (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | The measurement does not identify same-cell co-occurrence, regardless of mouse replication. It can inform a different sorted-population association question; that possible use is not refuted. Prior block retention remains in decision 30. |
| C147 | A Wnt-target module clears the per-cell detection floor that Axin2 alone does not, in the one deposit here with per-animal replication | feasibility measurement on six GSE262927 samples | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/module_detection_check/module_detection.csv` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High, and it reverses under three independent corrections the measurement did not control for. The module silently included Tcf7l2, which is the beta-catenin DNA-binding partner rather than a Wnt target gene and which was not in this project's own Route B Wnt set; without it the module falls below Axin2 alone in all six samples. The denominator is every barcode of six whole injured lungs rather than AT2 cells, which the artefact's own Sftpc detection of 5.9 to 36.8 per cent shows, and on Sftpc-positive barcodes Axin2 returns to the range row C138 already refused. And the six samples are six different conditions with no replicate pair among them, so the phrase about per-animal replication is false of the samples actually used. Row C151. |

| C148 | England et al. 2025's Figure S2 measurement is available for reanalysis in GSE247505 | trial A2: the superseries and both subseries contain zero mentions of ZsGreen, and the NCBI link service returns exactly three GEO series for that paper, none holding sorted Il1r1-lineage libraries | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/a2_does_il1r1_deletion_move_wnt/a2_summary.md` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High, with the reason corrected. **That figure is a three-gene bulk RT-qPCR panel, not a sequencing experiment**, so there were never libraries to deposit and the absence is not a withholding. The consequence for Route A is the same and the characterisation is not: this branch called a bulk three-gene panel the field's only direct measurement of overlap two rows after refusing Route C for being a bulk measurement, which is the asymmetry row C151 records. |
| C149 | The Il1r1 genotype labels of GSE247504 can be verified from the deposited matrices | trial A2 rule R2: Il1r1 per ten thousand is 0.202 and 0.162 in the two-week heterozygous libraries against 0.161 and 0.187 in the homozygous, and 0.199 and 0.095 against 0.200 and 0.122 at twelve weeks, interleaving at both timepoints | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/a2_does_il1r1_deletion_move_wnt/a2_libraries.csv` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | High for anyone reusing that deposit: the genotype labels rest on deposited metadata alone. **The mechanism this row previously asserted is withdrawn.** It claimed the floxed exons do not sit at the three-prime end, which the trial did not test and did not source; the allele is Il1r1-tm1.1Rbl, its floxed exons are 3 and 4, and Robson and colleagues report that excising them eliminates downstream exon expression, which is the opposite of the explanation offered. Why the deletion is invisible here is Not established. Row C151. |
| C150 | Deleting Il1r1 moves the Wnt programme in Kras-mutant AT2-derived clones | trial A2: not computable, because R2 refused the genotype labels. The unread values are recorded: at two weeks the Wnt module separates completely in the direction of more Wnt without Il1r1, at twelve weeks it does not, and with two libraries per group the smallest attainable permutation p is one in six | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/a2_does_il1r1_deletion_move_wnt/a2_readings.csv` | Not established (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30, as a block, to revisit) | Moderate. It is the untested half of the crosstalk Choi 2020 proposed, the reverse half is established by Aumiller 2013 and almost never cited, and this deposit cannot settle it |

| C151 | Rows C116 to C150 were written from the artefacts they cite | an eight-adversary audit of those rows, each finding sent to an independent verifier that defaulted to refuting it, returned 45 confirmed or partly confirmed defects across 14 rows. Four numbers were quoted that no run in this repository ever produced: the Sox9 corroboration axis of C116, two z values in C122, and all three figures in C127. Four more were wrong against the tables they cite. Two rows were internally inconsistent with their neighbours, one named the wrong laboratory, and one was refuted by this branch's own artefacts | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m4_the_corroboration_the_register_claimed/m4_summary.md` | Refuted (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | High, and it is the most useful result of the session. The trials are mostly honest and the register written from them was not, for one mechanical reason: summary prose was typed as string literals in scripts whose own index promises that summaries are regenerated from tracked tables. Every number in a summary must be formatted from the frame that holds it |
| C152 | A number without a traceable logged computation is not established as a repository result, even when it happens to be numerically correct | the audit distinguishes them, and so does this repository's own rule that a result with no logged artefact is Not established. Of the four unlogged numbers, the Sox9 ratio recomputes to 7.05 and was right; the C127 figures came from an adversarial agent and cannot be reproduced here at all | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m4_the_corroboration_the_register_claimed/m4_ratios.csv` | Descriptive only (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | High as the distinction the correction turned on. An unlogged number that happens to be right is still Not established at the moment it is quoted, because nothing in the repository could have caught it if it were wrong, and in three of the four cases nothing did This corrects the internal contradiction in the previous proposition that an unlogged number must be wrong. Decision 30 records the earlier block retention. |
| C153 | Trial M1c's labelling reproduces both source papers' reported directions and one reported ratio, not their absolute transitional fractions | trial M1c, produced and never registered until the audit found it missing: in GSE310539 the label rises from 0.33 and 0.22 per cent under PBS to 1.75 and 0.74 after Sendai, with the AP-1 mutant at 0.42 of the wildtype value against the 0.5 Lynch et al. report, and in the mature GSE247130 wells from 0.69 per cent in the infected control to 5.09 in the infected Cebpa mutant, which is the expansion Hassan and Chen report | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/datp_epigenetics/trials/m1c_label_at_the_depth_available/m1c_labels.csv` | Descriptive only (owner retained 2026-09-22, decision 30) | Moderate, and it should have been registered when it was produced. None of those comparisons is testable, because each condition is one library, but the label behaving as two independent papers describe is the reason the branch was willing to read anything from it |
| C154 | The Axin2–Il1r1 and Wnt-module association differs between transitional cells and AT2 cells | trial A1c, produced and never registered until the audit found it missing: in the one transitional set large enough to compute, 773 cells of the infected Cebpa mutant, the Axin2 against Il1r1 correlation flips positive at r = +0.070 and the Wnt module at +0.092, percentile 0.94 and 0.977, against negative values in every AT2 set | `Research Article/gate1_02_choi_2020/axin2_il1r1/trials/a1c_graded_statistic/a1c_pairs.csv` | Not established (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Moderate. Neither clears, the well is unreadable because its gate failed, and a difference between cell states is exactly what an underpowered design invents. It is registered because an unregistered result is invisible to every downstream reader, and because a later pass must not be able to present it as new The positive correlations in one infected Cebpa-mutant transitional set are descriptive readings from a failed gate; they do not establish a replicated state interaction. This replaces the bookkeeping proposition without promoting the result. Decision 30 records the earlier block retention. |

## How to keep this register honest

- Add a row only when a run record or decisions log exists for it.
- After changing a row's status, re-run `analysis/scripts/14_write_negative_results.py`;
  [`NEGATIVE_RESULTS.md`](NEGATIVE_RESULTS.md) is generated from this file and is
  never edited by hand.
- Quote numbers from the artefact in the row, never from memory.
- When the owner rejects a row, change its status to Retracted-superseded and
  leave it in place; record the rejection in DEVELOPMENT.md.
- A row's Potential is a judgement, and says so; it does not change the status.

## Displaced (established elsewhere)

Rows moved out of the Stage 0 table on 2026-09-10 because the result is
established outside this repository (weighted
toward the Krt8-high transitional state). The artefacts stay in place (since
2026-09-21 under `Research Article/`, beside their source papers), the scripts under
`analysis/scripts/`, and both remain validated; the narrative is not extended
here. The row keeps its number so that cross-references elsewhere still
resolve.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C2 | The AT2 to Krt8-high transitional to AT1 ordering is recovered, and the transitional state is a true intermediate (median per-animal 27.4% at 11 dpi, 0.3% at 366 dpi) | regeneration focus: alveolar subset, PAGA plus diffusion pseudotime rooted in AT2, labels held out | [`archive/DISPLACED.md`](archive/DISPLACED.md) (artefacts unchanged under `Research Article/gate1_01_niethamer_2025/GSE262927/regeneration_focus/`) | Validated (qualifier 2026-09-29: recovery with deposited labels held out is internal concordance on the same RNA, and pseudotime is an inferred ordering; true intermediate refers to ordering position and abundance, not demonstrated lineage passage) | Not extended here; established outside this repository. The stem-niche entry (branch 2N) in the trial plan rests on the proliferation figure, not on this trajectory. |

## Cross-study epithelial specificity: ES1 correction, 2026-09-22

These are post-audit descriptive results from frozen source definitions on
previously inspected datasets. Technical seeds do not add biological replicates.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C165 | The ADI published-marker contrast of the two-transcript-labelled group depends on developmental and injury context in the examined control wells | ES1 at 2,000 RNA UMI, label-free published ADI list: P9 control +1.04/+1.21 detection points; adult SeV control +7.61/+6.53 across seeds 17/29. All 400 source markers retained before exclusion of label genes; 398 remain and 390 are present in the multiome universe | `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/results/within_unit_effects.csv`; `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/modules.json` | Descriptive only (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | A within-well conditional contrast, one pooled library per condition. Higher absolute neonatal scores and a smaller labelled-reference difference are compatible. This does not establish a developmental mechanism, injury specificity, full DATP/PATS equivalence or fate. |
| C166 | ES1 has replicated external biological confirmation under the frozen two-group eligibility rule | GSE262927: 25 animals examined, only EEM-scRNA-167 qualifies in both seeds (40/193 and 35/197 labelled/reference cells); ADI contrasts +12.64/+11.58 detection points. The 7-week multiome control also crosses its floor between seeds (30 versus 26 labelled cells) | `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/results/retention.csv`; `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/results/within_unit_effects.csv` | Not established (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | The external study was already analyzed by this repository and lacks a neonatal arm. One eligible mouse provides a consistency observation, not replicated confirmation. Failure of a cell floor is not biological absence. |
| C167 | The published AT2-marker labelled-reference contrast is smaller in the sampled Cebpa-mutant wells than in controls at the same stage | ES1 label-free AT2 list: P9 controls -4.59/-4.79 detection points versus mutants -0.70/-0.31; injured adult controls -8.88/-7.34 versus mutants -2.28/-2.43. Reference scores are also lower in mutants | `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/results/within_unit_effects.csv`; `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/results/between_well_contrasts.csv` | Descriptive only (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | One well per genotype/stage and reference selection on Sftpc prevent an inferred genotype-by-state interaction. This motivates testing whether baseline reference shifts or labelled-cell changes explain the contrast, with animal replication and independent state definitions. |
| C168 | Broad ADI and AT1 marker scores are not independent evidence for transitional identity and mature AT1 fate | The source-derived ADI and AT1 holdout lists share 119 genes; the four-gene late-AT1 panel changes direction across technical seeds in several injured wells and the one eligible external mouse | `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/results/module_overlap.csv`; `Research Article/epithelial_state_specificity/results/seed_sensitivity.csv` | Descriptive only (delegated reassessment 2026-09-22, decision 32) | Shared membership is an arithmetic dependence, not proof that shared genes cause transition. Split shared and AT1-unique components and compare with independent late-maturation endpoints before reading an AT1 score as fate. |

## Reference atlases: Nabhan, Sikkema and Murthy packages, registered 2026-09-29

Results from the three reference-atlas packages that had no row of their own
(the Nabhan source reproduction and Nb1, Sikkema trials S1 to S5, and the Murthy
decision log). Most are annotation and QC records rather than biological findings;
they are registered so that later work citing these cohorts inherits their caveats.
Cells, clusters and captures in these rows are not biological replicates.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C169 | Nabhan et al.'s statement that 74% of Wnt5a-positive adult lung mesenchymal cells express Pdgfra is reproduced qualitatively but not numerically from the deposited FPKM matrix | GSE109444, 47 deposited single-cell FPKM profiles (bulk control GSM2943054 excluded), four fixed positivity rules applied to both genes: Pdgfra among Wnt5a-positive 28/31 (90.3%) at >0 and >=0.1, 21/26 (80.8%) at >=1, 13/18 (72.2%) at >=5; Wnt5a is the most frequently detected Wnt (26/47 at >=1). Retrospective specification written after reading the methods and one input file Run record: `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/README.md`. | `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/tables/conditional_codetection.csv`; `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/summary.json`; `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/tables/detection_by_threshold.csv` | Descriptive only (source-data reproduction; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The published threshold and filtering are unstated, so the gap to 74% cannot be attributed. The 47 cells have no animal mapping and are not 47 replicates. Transcript co-detection is not ligand secretion, niche proximity or the paper's perturbation evidence. Nothing further to run without the authors' filtering rule. |
| C170 | The deposited Nabhan mesenchymal single-cell profiles carry Sftpc transcripts in almost every cell, an unresolved carryover or identity caveat | GSE109444 at FPKM >=1: Sftpc detected in 46/47 cells (median 11.2 FPKM) alongside Col1a2, Col1a1 and Mgp in 47/47; Epcam in 4, Pecam1, Ptprc and Ager in 0; all 47 pass the recoverable three-of-four housekeeping diagnostic Run record: `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/README.md`. | `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/tables/single_cell_fpkm.csv`; `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/tables/detection_by_threshold.csv`; `Research Article/gate1_03_nabhan_2018/source_reproduction/tables/housekeeping_qc_diagnostic.csv` | Descriptive only (method caution; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The matrix cannot distinguish epithelial carryover, mixed-cell material or biological expression; it does not establish doublets or justify relabelling. Any reuse of these cells as a pure fibroblast source should carry the caveat. |

Suggests asking whether AF1- and AF2-adjacent AT2 cells sit in different Wnt niches, which needs spatial data. |

## Cardoso trial C6, registered 2026-09-29

Trial C6 has a run record and a summary, and rows C38 and C83 refer to its
result, but no row registered it. One library per genotype; nothing here is
replicated.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C195 | In the mouse Kras tumour niche libraries Hbegf is detected in non-epithelial compartments, chiefly endothelial-gated cells, and after Areg deletion its detection is retained in endothelial and mesenchymal gates but not in myeloid gates | Trial C6, marker gates, library as unit, no P value: non-epithelial Hbegf detection >=0.1 in endothelial gates of Red2Kras niche 0.2137, Areg-flox/+ niche 0.2432 and Areg-flox/flox niche 0.1842, mesenchymal 0.1143 and 0.1135, neutrophil 0.1783 (flox/+). Areg-flox/flox against flox/+ retained fraction 0.757 endothelial and 0.818 mesenchymal against 0.306 alveolar macrophage-like, 0.35 neutrophil and 0.429 other myeloid (rule: survives if >=0.7). Tested hypothesis: Hbegf as an Areg-independent driver Run record: `Research Article/gate2_05_cardoso_2026/trials/c6_who_makes_egfr_ligands/c6_run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_05_cardoso_2026/trials/c6_who_makes_egfr_ligands/c6_ligands_by_compartment.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_05_cardoso_2026/trials/c6_who_makes_egfr_ligands/c6_hbegf_survival.csv` | Descriptive only (source pattern); driver reading not established (delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | One library per genotype, marker gates are not cell-type calls and depth differs between libraries; detection retention is not an Areg-independent driver. The endothelial gate in the RFP-sorted epithelial libraries (0.89 and 0.7517) needs a gating check before reuse. Human sources differ (C38, C45), but species, disease and deposit are confounded. Related rows: C33, C38, C43, C83. |

## Question-level results: programmes and outcomes (A0, A5, A5/A11, A10, A11), registered 2026-09-29

First register rows for question-specific results. RESEARCH_QUESTIONS.md is
the question index, and a question-level interpretation does not inherit a paper
row's status. The owner asked on 29 September 2026 for every claim across the
paper packages and RQ_Specified to be registered. Each status below is a grade
proposed under that request and tagged as a delegated reassessment; it stands
until the owner retains or rejects it (DEVELOPMENT.md). The Strunz mice used by A0
and A5 are the same animals, and the A10 unit is the well, not an independent
preparation.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C196 | A 50-gene RNA programme selected to exceed both reference states in lung repair (Strunz, Krt8+ ADI against AT2 and AT1) and in early human airway development (Sountoulidis, epi_cl1 against epi_cl2 and epi_cl0) scores higher in the intermediate than in both endpoints in every discovery context | A0 pilot_v1, prospectively frozen configuration; 11,590 one-to-one orthologs; 134 genes met the four-contrast rule (median >= 0.1 log2 CPM, >= two-thirds positive units), 50 retained. Frozen percentile-rank score, paired intermediate-minus-endpoint medians (score points x100): D1 9 mice +3.7170 vs AT2 (9/9), +2.7349 vs AT1 (9/9); D2 11 donors +3.3167 vs distal (10/11), +3.7912 vs proximal (11/11); smallest leave-one-out medians +3.6460, +2.6250, +3.2554, +3.7213. D1 equal-day means +3.6794 and +2.6700 Run record: `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/reports/PILOT_V1_RESULTS.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/transfer_summary.tsv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/transfer_unit_differences.tsv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/discovery_gate_counts.tsv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/frozen_programme.json` | Exploratory (selected and scored in the same discovery units; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The discovery-context differences are selected estimates, not confirmation of a conserved programme. Specificity (stress, cycling, depth, matched random modules) was pruned by the pre-declared stop rule, so generic activation, source labels and dissociation remain unresolved. D2 is a human airway branch, not alveolar maturation. The Strunz mice are the same animals used by A5 and the A0 exploratory run, so these are not independent cohorts. |
| C197 | The frozen A0 50-gene programme transfers to intestinal differentiation, peaking in late enterocyte progenitors relative to both stem cells and mature proximal enterocytes | A0 pilot_v1 P3, Haber GSE92332, 3 verified control mice (1,013 primary cells): intermediate minus stem median +0.2694 score points (2/3 positive, smallest leave-one-out +0.0786, pass); intermediate minus mature enterocyte median -0.0914 (1/3 positive, smallest leave-one-out -0.2929, fail). Per mouse vs mature: +0.4065, -0.0914, -0.4945 (Control-Mouse1, 3, 4). Decision STOP_PRIMARY_TRANSFER_NOT_SUPPORTED; P4 specificity pruned by the rule committed before V1 scores Run record: `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/reports/PILOT_V1_RESULTS.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/transfer_summary.tsv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/transfer_unit_differences.tsv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/transfer_leave_one_out.tsv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/pilot_v1/pilot_status.json` | Not established (the pre-declared transfer criterion failed against mature enterocytes; three mice cannot establish absence; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | A negative result on the simplest transferable-signature version of the hypothesis, reached by a rule fixed before scoring. With three mice it is a directional criterion, not an equivalence test; it cannot separate tissue specificity from differences in branch, species, platform, source QC or state definitions. Broader conservation and causal fate modulation remain open and would need a separately justified design. |
| C198 | In lung repair a 50-gene RNA programme distinguishes author-labelled Krt8+ ADI cells from both AT2 and AT1 cells of the same mouse, and the difference survives within-mouse depth matching, endpoint-mixture and matched random-set checks | A0 repair-first exploratory run (separate from pilot_v1), GSE141259 days 10-15, 9 mice, 886 eligible genes, top 50. Leave-one-mouse-out refits: 9/9 mice positive, median +0.0794 vs AT2 and +0.0624 vs AT1; fold Jaccard 0.639-0.786. UMI-quintile matching 9/9 positive, +0.0629 and +0.0574. All 9 exceed simulated AT2/AT1 mixtures at 25/50/75% weights (median worst gap +0.0503). 0 of 200 matched random sets reach the candidate median. 19 of 50 genes are in the generic-control union; the other 31 remain positive in 9/9 mice (median +0.0679 vs AT2, +0.0565 vs AT1) Run record: `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/reports/EXPLORATORY_PILOT_REPORT.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/stage_E1_result.json`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/stage_E2_result.json`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/exploratory/repair_heldout_effects.csv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/exploratory/repair_depth_matched_effects.csv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/exploratory/repair_endpoint_mixture_checks.csv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/exploratory/program_genes.csv`; `RQ_Specified/A0_conserved_epithelial_transition_program/tables/exploratory/repair_control_effects.csv` | Exploratory (one cohort, author state labels, selection and evaluation in the same mice; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | A stable within-dataset repair association. Author labels are reused, so it does not independently validate the transitional state or its fate. Matched-set tail fractions are conditional diagnostics, not selection-adjusted p-values; synthetic mixtures do not exhaust doublets or ambient RNA; the low-cycle restriction leaves only one mouse at the floor, so independence from proliferation is weakly tested. Same Strunz mice as A5 and pilot_v1 D1. |

## Question-level results: recipient competence (A6, A9, A12, A13), registered 2026-09-29

Question-level results for A6, A9, A12 and A13, graded on the same delegated
basis as the section above. The A12 endpoint is general TNF/NF-kB inflammatory
RNA, not an IL-1-specific readout, and every model comparison reuses the same 12
paired patients.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C221 | In GSE308103 LUAD-minus-matched-normal patient differences, adding an equal-weight recipient RNA index (mean IL1R1/IL1RAP minus mean IL1R2/IL1RN/SIGIRR, log2(1+CPM)) in the fixed healthy-reference AT2 candidate label to a ridge model already holding source IL1A/IL1B, monocyte-derived macrophage fraction and TNF lowered leave-one-patient-out error for a predictor-disjoint TNF/NF-kB hallmark RNA endpoint. The endpoint is general inflammatory transcription, not an IL-1-specific readout | A12 exploratory pilot, specification frozen before scores/fits after recorded prior exposure; 12 paired patients at the 50-cell floor, confidence 0.2, ridge alpha=1; endpoint 193 of 197 frozen genes assayed (ICOSLG, IL6ST, RIGI, SIK1 absent, not zero-filled). Held-out RMSE: training mean 0.376284, source 0.518922, source+TNF 0.427139, joint 0.324373; MSE improvement 0.07723; joint Q2 against training mean 0.256882; 7/12 patients with lower error. Same ordering at alpha 0.1 (0.462237 to 0.338203), alpha 10 (0.345462 to 0.315958), 30-cell floor n=18 (0.301674 to 0.276102) and confidence 0.3 n=16 (0.324600 to 0.296035), the same patients re-used. No interval or p value: LOPO fold losses share training observations Run record: `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_PILOT.md`. | `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_model_metrics.csv`; `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_heldout_predictions.csv`; `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_pilot_results.json`; `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_pilot_specification.json`; `RQ_Specified/A12_recipient_context/figures/figure_run.json` | Exploratory (single cohort, 12 patients, reused data, unreplicated; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The source-only model is worse than predicting the training mean (0.518922 against 0.376284), so the gain is measured against a comparator that needed TNF to become useful; the statement is about one three-term model, not about source RNA in general. RNA index is not receptor protein, occupancy or reception; the endpoint cannot show action through IL-1. Leading rivals: a shared disease inflammation axis, within-AT2-label composition, shared full-library normalization, unadjudicated AT2 label correspondence, assigned-source conditionality (A12-S1) and two high-leverage patients (separate row). External replication is blocked (separate row); no serialized fit exists for transport. IL-1 selectivity needs a selective perturbation, not another RNA model. |
| C222 | In the A12 AT2 arm the recipient index has a modest marginal association with the inflammatory endpoint that is concentrated in two patients, and the saved held-out predictions span a wider range than the observations; the earlier caption claiming range compression was false | 12 paired patients, primary settings. The slopes are prediction-on-observation descriptions and do not by themselves locate failures at the extremes (2026-09-29 figure audit, A12 F03). A replicate cohort with at least 10 eligible pairs, or a leave-two-out description, would show whether the gain survives without these patients. |

Direction changes across sensitivities: worse at alpha 0.1 (0.214368 to 0.239995) and the 30-cell floor (0.348467 to 0.365944), better at alpha 10 (0.254716 to 0.248171) and confidence 0.3 (0.420199 to 0.418146). Against source only the index would look favourable (0.407185 to 0.364795) Run record: `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_PILOT.md`. | `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_model_metrics.csv`; `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_heldout_predictions.csv`; `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-27-followthrough/A12_pilot_results.json` | Not established (inconclusive: direction unstable across predeclared sensitivities; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | An inconclusive estimate, not evidence against fibroblast recipient context. ## England 2025 and CD177 attribution, registered 2026-09-29

The England batch1, continuation, follow-up and A16 attribution results, graded
on the same delegated basis as the sections above. Two limits govern the whole
family. Biological pool, reporter pair and processing batch are unknown for all
20 GSE247505 libraries (C234), so every England RNA estimate is library-level and
none can be Validated. And every follow-up and A16 endpoint was computed on data
whose earlier results had already been seen (C269), so none of it is independent
validation. The only Validated row here is C253, an implementation fact about
deposited simulator code with no animal unit.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C234 | The 20 GSE247505 libraries cannot be resolved to animals, biological pools or reporter pairs, so every England RNA estimate in this package is a library-level description | EN0 intake ledger over the whole deposit: 20 GEO libraries, 68 ENA runs and 20 BioSamples (runs are sequencing records of the same library, not extra mice); biological pool id, reporter pair id, processing batch and verified independence known for 0 of 20 libraries; the paper states 13 comparative Experiment-1 libraries against 10 deposited GSMs and no deposit or public record explains the difference. One identical feature table for all libraries, 31,053 genes, 36 duplicated symbols summed, non-negative integer counts, no duplicate barcodes within a library and no reporter (RFP/YFP) features present. Source-style QC keeps 44,196 cells (Experiment 1 32,698, Experiment 2 11,498); primary_include 41,811, strict_include 39,583 Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/continuation/prep/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/continuation/prep/EN0_availability_ledger.json`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/continuation/prep/matrix_checks.json`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/metadata/analysis_unit_map.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/SOURCE_AUDIT.md` | Descriptive only (data-availability decision record; the arithmetic is inside the continuation's 1,178 passed checks; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | This is the ceiling on every other England row: with no pool or pair identity, no p value, confidence interval or animal-level inference is available from the RNA, and RFP/YFP libraries cannot be treated as paired mice. It does not show that the pooling is unbalanced, only that it is unknown. Author-supplied pool/animal/pair identities would convert most library ranges here into testable contrasts Related rows: C19, C149. |

The same branch is inert in both q=0.5 blocks (KS 0.000). Seed-to-seed KS distances are <=0.0078, so these are not Monte Carlo fluctuations, and the Gillespie implementation reproduces the analytic one-lineage birth-death law (extinction-probability error <=0.00115, conditional CCDF error <=0.00703). Mutant RFP full support: 1 week single geometric 2.8231, mixture 2.4806, negative binomial 2.5170 (4 mice); 2 weeks 5.4142, 4.0425, 4.0015 (4 mice, negative binomial better in 3 of 4 held-out mice); 4 weeks 6.4348, 4.1260, 4.0361 (3 mice, better in 3 of 3). After within-mouse upper-1% trimming the late ranking holds (2 weeks 5.0941 / 3.9038 / 3.8863; 4 weeks 5.7059 / 3.9034 / 3.8640). The verification independently recomputed all 246 held-out likelihoods with scipy.stats Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/clones/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/clones/model_LOMO_summary.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/clones/model_LOMO_by_mouse.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/verification.json` | Refuted as a claim of unique support for two founder classes (held-out comparison across mice; the biological hypothesis itself is not refuted; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | These are distributional competitors, not a rerun of the source's stochastic two-compartment simulation, and a continuous alternative winning on held-out likelihood does not establish a continuous biological hierarchy any more than the mixture established two immutable cell types. The source's lineage evidence for founder differences is independent of this comparison. The discriminating analysis is A17's corrected-implementation refit scored on the same held-out bins and folds as the continuous comparator, with a separately frozen tail diagnostic |
| C257 | Fitted mixture components are cohort-dependent in mutant clones and not identifiable at all in homeostatic clones | EN6 leave-one-mouse-out parameter spread on the batch1 fits. Mutant RFP two-component mixtures have non-overlapping components in every held-out fit, but the slow-component geometric parameter shifts by cohort: 0.0374-0.0714 with weight 0.143-0.276 at 1 week, 0.0038-0.0047 with weight 0.301-0.339 at 2 weeks and 0.0009-0.0011 with weight 0.222-0.247 at 4 weeks. Across the six Confetti cohorts mixture weights span 0.085-0.886 and components overlap in five of six, i.e. the two components are not separable there Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/continuation/EN6/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/continuation/EN6/lomo_parameter_spread.csv` | Descriptive only (fit-stability description across held-out mice; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Sets the identifiability bar for A17: a fitted mixture whose components move an order of magnitude between harvest cohorts and collapse in homeostatic tissue cannot be read as two stable cell types with fixed kinetics. It does not show the components are artefacts, only that these data do not pin them. Parameter identifiability should be reported alongside any refit |
| C258 | The corrected-implementation stochastic refit that would decide the founder-class question has not been run, and its existing contract is not executable as frozen | The follow-up contract specifies FU_S as 560 parameter points x 20 replicates x 2,000 clones x 2 implementations and it was not executed. Recorded amendment requirements before execution: state whether rate changes and switching times are fixed from the archive, re-estimated or removed; reconcile manuscript parameter symbols with the code (Methods S1 Table 3 f_S=0.16 and 1.1 per week against archive fs=0.08 and 0.9 per week); give the negative-binomial comparator the same held-out bins and training folds; and freeze a separate tail diagnostic, because the source used log-CCDF RMSLE while FU_S proposes a binned likelihood and a terminal 51-plus bin can hide the upper tail that distinguishes the models. Input accounting is complete: the primary RFP input is 11 source-indexed mice and 16,113 clones of size >=2 (4, 4 and 3 mice at 1, 2 and 4 weeks), and a fresh decode reproduced all 58 batch1 mouse/analysis-channel count rows Run record: `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-28-gap-fill/a17_source_accounting/REPORT.md`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/config/followup_contract.json`; `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-28-gap-fill/a17_source_accounting/primary_cohort_manifest.csv`; `docs/roadmap_runs/2026-09-28-gap-fill/a17_source_accounting/REPORT.md` | Not established (not executed; the decision to defer and the required amendment are recorded; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Feasible with existing inputs and no new data, and the only England analysis in this package that could still change a biological conclusion without new experiments. Anyone reusing Table S1 as the mouse inventory would undercount animals at 1 and 2 weeks. Author confirmation of the printed table is the resolution |
| C260 | The shared repair programme rises transiently and reverses in both external repair datasets, as those studies themselves report | EN7 unit-level pseudobulks, log2(CPM+1) differences. Niethamer 2025 (two animals per day): day 11 minus day 0 gives cycling +3.655, transition RNA +2.946, shared +1.439, lesion +0.390 and Itga2 +2.756, with all four cross-unit directions agreeing, and day 25 minus day 11 reverses them; Cd177 is -0.327 with disagreeing directions at the peak. Choi contributes one pooled library per condition, so only the Niethamer arm has any animal replication and it has two animals per day. It is not independent validation of anything in England, and no injury-to-cancer axis is measured |
| C261 | The CD177 contrast cannot be tested in either repair dataset because Cd177-positive transitional cells are essentially absent there | EN7 with the same transition gate and 30-per-side floor: Choi day 14 has 117 transition-gated cells of 3,787 with 2 Cd177-detected, day 28 has 21 of 3,572 with 1, and PBS has none of 4,315; across the 25 Niethamer samples transition-gated counts run 0-50 with at most 1 Cd177-detected cell. Every unit returns unavailable.
