| C262 | No direction, rate or reversibility of state conversion can be estimated from this deposit | The follow-up contract excluded optimal-transport couplings and any directed Markov chain on a pseudotime or similarity kernel before execution, on the ground that their arrows follow from the kernel and root choice. RNA velocity is not computable: GEO supplies CellRanger filtered count matrices with no spliced/unspliced layers, and the only route is re-quantifying the 68 ENA runs, which needs remote compute and separate authorization. Accordingly the follow-up reproduces the paper's pipeline shape (two-round subclustering, contaminant removal on Ptprc/Pecam1/Col1a1/Foxj1, 19 Experiment-1 clusters over 31,387 cells and 15 Experiment-2 clusters over 9,399 after discarding 4 clusters/1,311 cells and 10 clusters/2,099 cells) and tests predictions of the transition model rather than measuring transitions. No figure in the package carries a transition arrow. The inspected article and supplements document Seurat clustering, integration, signatures, correlations and CellChat and no computational directional trajectory method Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/config/followup_contract.json`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_E_round2_clusters.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_E_round1_contaminant_waterfall.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_E_diffusion_ordering.csv` | Not establishable with this deposit (prospective exclusion recorded in the frozen contract; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The single most load-bearing constraint on the England reading: every 'transition' statement in this package is a statement about state occupancy, not about movement. Two Experiment-2 clusters (24 and 74 cells) are disconnected from the diffusion root component and carry infinite values, so even the ordering is partial. Spliced/unspliced quantification from the 68 ENA runs is the only route to directional evidence from this deposit; lineage recording with a resolved AT1 endpoint is the route to fate |
| C263 | Graph topology and composition drift cannot distinguish a reversibly mixing state pool from a static continuum in these libraries | FU_T1 PAGA connectivity among mutant-dominated round-2 clusters (mutant fraction above 0.5 on both sides) exceeds 0.1 in 12 of 21 pairs in Experiment 1 and 30 of 91 in Experiment 2. FU_T3 total variation in state composition between timepoints is 0.2887 (Experiment 1, day 4 to day 14) and 0.3356 (Experiment 2, day 14 to day 84), against within-timepoint replicate-library variation of 0.3522 and 0.2922 in Experiment 1 and 0.1407 and 0.4790 in Experiment 2; neither between-timepoint value exceeds the larger within-timepoint value, though the Experiment-2 value does exceed its smaller one (the results prose quotes only the larger value per experiment, 0.35 and 0.48) Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_T1_paga_connectivity.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_T3_composition_stationarity.csv` | Not established (uninformative between the competing descriptions; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | PAGA is undirected, so a connected graph is equally compatible with rapid mixing and with a static continuum, and library-to-library heterogeneity swamps the time signal, so drift and stationarity cannot be separated. Registered to stop a connected state graph being read as evidence for reversibility. Time-resolved lineage recording, or libraries with pool identities so that within-timepoint variation can be estimated properly, would make the comparison meaningful |

It is the RNA analogue of the source's double-positive staining, not a substitute for it |
| C265 | Depth-matched cycling-marker differences between the AT2 and mixed states are near zero across mutant libraries, and the other state pairs are underpowered | FU_T4 over mutant libraries with at least three usable depth strata. AT2 against transition gives a median of +0.357 over 4 libraries (individual values 0.6283, 0.5510, 0.1627, -0.2405) and transition against mixed -0.295 over 4 (-0.8123, -0.4475, -0.1418, +0.4798), both inconsistent in sign Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_T4_cycling_equipotency.csv` | Descriptive only (consistent with the source's comparable-potential claim for one state pair; underpowered for the others; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Cycling-marker RNA is not a proliferation assay and the paper measured proliferative potential with organoids, transplantation and EdU, so agreement here is corroboration of a direction at best. A near-zero median across 12 libraries with a 6/6 sign split is also exactly what no effect and no power look like. Sorted-state EdU or Ki67 with clone output is the measurement |
| C266 | Wild-type neighbour clone size falls with distance from a mutant clone in oncogenic tissue, on a multiplicative scale where a bounded readout cannot produce the gradient | FU_W on the deposited pooled spatial pair arrays (bins require at least ten pair rows and a midpoint within 400 micrometres; slopes are pair-row weighted). Percent-of-distal-level size slopes per 100 micrometres: kras1w -108.9, kras2w -78.0, kras4w -41.5 and kras4d -16.2, against nine homeostatic Confetti datasets spanning -22.1 to +0.2; restricted to bins with at least 100 pair rows the values are -108.6, -69.4 and -42.8 against conf60w at -55.5, so only the 1- and 2-week timepoints separate from homeostatic tissue on both estimators. On a log scale the mean absolute slope per 100 micrometres is 0.435 in oncogenic tissue (0.754, 0.526, 0.310, 0.150) against 0.062 in homeostatic tissue. Near and far mean neighbour sizes are 4.88 and 2.77 at 1 week on 7,576 pair rows. Size slopes are negative in 12 of 13 datasets Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_W_run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_W_distance_slopes.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/clones/spatial_pooled_bin_profiles.csv` | Descriptive only (reproduction of the source's own wild-type expansion result on pooled rows; no mouse unit; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The value added over the source is that the gradient survives a scale objection, since an unbounded count was being compared with a fraction bounded at 1, and that its four-week separation depends on the estimator. It cannot support causality, significance or a mouse-level effect: pooled rows carry no mouse or clone identifiers, one neighbour may contribute several rows, pair rows collapse with distance (kras1w 4,953 rows in the 0-50 micrometre bin to 13 at 200-250), and clone merger near large lesions would inflate short-distance neighbour size with no change in division rate |
| C267 | The pro-Sftpc-negative fraction shows no consistent distance dependence in either oncogenic or homeostatic tissue, which is not the same as independence of proximity | FU_W, same bins and weighting. Percent-of-distal-level pro-Sftpc-negative slopes per 100 micrometres are -49.3, +11.9, +3.5 and -2.5 in the four oncogenic datasets and -12.8 to +40.1 across the nine homeostatic ones; on a logit scale the mean absolute slope is 0.185 oncogenic (values -0.507, +0.155, -0.037, +0.039) against 0.155 homeostatic, and the sign split across all 13 datasets is 8 positive to 5 negative. The fraction itself is raised two- to fivefold over homeostatic tissue at every distance (oncogenic near 0.137-0.277 against homeostatic near 0.032-0.093). kras1w is the only dataset carrying both gradients (-0.754 log size, -0.507 logit fraction) Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_W_run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/followup/FU_W_distance_slopes.csv` | Descriptive only (reproduction of the source's decoupling comparison; an absent slope in a noisy readout is not a demonstration of independence; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | England et al. state the decoupling directly, so this block is a reproduction and not an independent contrast, and its exposure is declared: the slopes were computed as scoping before the block was frozen. The pro-Sftpc-negative fraction already sits near 0.2-0.3 close to the clone, which leaves less room to rise, distance in Confetti tissue is clone-to-clone rather than clone-to-mutant, and fractions of 0.2-0.3 are not by themselves evidence of saturation. Two separate mediators are not identified by a distance-by-readout contrast |
| C268 | Animal-level spatial inference on the wild-type response near mutant clones is blocked by the deposited arrays, not by analysis choices | The batch1 spatial identity gate over all 13 datasets records mouse_id_available and unique_clone_id_available as false for every one, and every bin profile row carries biological_inference_allowed false. Pair rows collapse with distance (kras1w 4,953 rows at 0-50 micrometres, 348 at 100-150, 40 at 150-200, 13 at 200-250 and 1-7 beyond 250), which the source methods themselves name as limiting power, and one neighbour clone may contribute to several rows. The joint spatial analysis EN6c is recorded as blocked for the same reason, while mouse-indexed non-spatial clone arrays do exist and support the narrower clone-composition questions Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/clones/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/clones/spatial_identity_gate.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/clones/spatial_pooled_bin_profiles.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/CANDIDATE_HYPOTHESES.md` | Not establishable without mouse and clone identifiers (blocking decision recorded before and after execution; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Defines exactly what to request: mouse, central-clone and neighbour-clone identifiers for the pair arrays, which the source holds and did not deposit. Until then no distance effect, no significance and no tumour-promoting versus tumour-restraining reading of the wild-type response is available, and no new RNA fit can supply the missing identifiers. The identity block applies to the pooled spatial arrays only, not to every England clonal estimate |
| C269 | Every England follow-up and A16 endpoint was computed on data whose earlier results had already been seen, so none of it can serve as independent validation | Declared exposure chain: batch1 was frozen at commit c82b0e05 before its outcomes were evaluated; the continuation contract and amendments CA1-CA3 were frozen before the continuation ran; the follow-up contract was frozen at commit 71f8ba0 after every batch1 and continuation endpoint had been reported; the FU_W slopes were computed as scoping before the FU_W block was frozen; A16 Stage 1 reuses the same processed matrices as FU_C, whose residual motivated the question, and its population erratum was written before any endpoint was computed; the corrected C1 specification was committed at e1d46b4f before corrected outcomes. Numerical verification is separate and passed: 6,489 checks with maximum arithmetic discrepancy 3.1e-14 in batch1, 1,178 checks at 1.8e-15 in the continuation, and corrected-C1 recomputation agreeing to 4.44e-16 Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/RESULTS_FOLLOWUP.md`. | `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/BATCH1_CONTRACT.md`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/CONTINUATION_CONTRACT.md`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/config/followup_contract.json`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/batch1/verification.json`; `Research Article/gate2_C2_england_2025/trials/continuation/verification.json`; `RQ_Specified/A16_cd177_state_attribution/correction_20260928/tables/correction_verification.json` | Descriptive only (exposure and verification decision record; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | This is why no England or A16 row in this register can be graded Validated on these data whatever its numbers look like, and it is recorded so that a later pass cannot present any of it as confirmation. Verification checks arithmetic, joins and recorded bytes; it does not check biological validity, pool independence or state identity. An independent test requires a deposit or experiment not yet inspected |

## Yu, Lee, Choi and Min 2026, registered 2026-09-29

The Yu, Lee, Choi and Min package. Its source is a review with no dataset, so
no row here reproduces a source result (C295): the review supplies the
organizing hypothesis and every measurement comes from independently identified
primary deposits. Two of its outputs were already registered through A12 as C225
(unassigned IL1B RNA) and C226 (the human pathway family), and are not repeated.
C293 is the methodological row the pathway results must be read beside: whether
this package reports any pathway discovery is decided by the inter-gene
correlation assumption.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C270 | The ten deposited series behind the review's proposed tests carry 371 sample records, but only one deposit contains an anti-IL-1beta intervention arm and it holds three treated against four control libraries at its early endpoint | trial U0, deposited GEO metadata only, no matrix opened: 371 sample records over ten series (GSE141259 60, GSE222901 35, GSE267226 5, GSE267228 4, GSE277777 32, GSE300288 31, GSE300293 19, GSE307529 54, GSE307534 56, GSE308103 75); GSE300288 at 3 months post-NNK has 4 Control IgG and 3 anti-IL-1beta deposited libraries, every other treatment/endpoint group 4; GSE267228 deposits anti-CD8 and IgG, not anti-IL-1beta; GSE308103 has 23 patient labels of which 22 carry normal, precursor and LUAD; GSE307534 has 25 labels, 24 precursor/LUAD pairs and 1 normal/precursor/LUAD Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u0_geo_design_audit/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u0_geo_design_audit/sample_inventory.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u0_geo_design_audit/GSE300288_group_counts.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u0_geo_design_audit/human_pairing_coverage.csv` | Descriptive only (deposited-metadata design audit; no expression measured; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Moderate as a planning constraint: it fixes the largest attainable intervention design in public data at 4 against 3 libraries and shows the human normal/precursor/LUAD pairing lives in one study. Pairings are title-derived and not a verified specimen crosswalk, and library counts are not animal counts. The discriminating next step is obtaining the authors' animal and capture crosswalk. |
| C271 | Every deposited GSE300288 record carries a formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded source characteristic beside single-cell Cell Ranger processing metadata, and none resolves animal identity or pool size | trial U0 design table over all 31 GSE300288 records: assay_metadata_conflict True in 31/31, animal_id 'unresolved' in 31/31, pool_size 'unresolved' in 31/31, file_triple_present True in 31/31; the retrieved MINiML record carries the characteristic 'cell line: Formalin-fixed paraffin embedded mouse lung tissue' and Cell Ranger data processing in all 31 sample entries Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u0_geo_design_audit/run_record.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u0_geo_design_audit/GSE300288_design.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u0_geo_design_audit/GSE300288.json` | Descriptive only (metadata contradiction preserved, not resolved; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Low biologically, load-bearing procedurally: the published methods support a fresh single-cell suspension reading, so the deposited characteristic is most likely a metadata error, but it is unresolved here and it blocks any assay-specific correction. It does not by itself invalidate the counts, which passed the U1 input checks. Resolve by author enquiry before any claim that depends on fixation chemistry or ambient RNA behaviour. |
| C272 | The prespecified primary endpoint, the KAC fraction response to anti-IL-1beta, cannot be estimated from the public deposits | trial U2 source audit: Zenodo record 17172149 (LungPCA_Code) returns access_right 'restricted' with an empty file list, and the author Figure 7 plotting code loads an external annotated object rather than building annotations; trial U4 denominator gate at the declared 100-alveolar-cell floor retains 3 of 4 Control IgG and 2 of 3 anti-IL-1beta animals, below the declared three per arm; the 50-cell sensitivity retains 4 and 3 animals but supplies no KAC definition, and the 200-cell floor retains 3 and 1 Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u4_mouse_niche/validation.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u2_source_audit/run_record.json`; `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u4_mouse_niche/alveolar_fraction_eligibility.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u4_mouse_niche/validation.json` | Not establishable (author classifier restricted and eligible animals insufficient; this is missing evidence, not evidence of no effect; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | High as a gate: it is the one endpoint the review's central proposal most needs, and no cell-floor change recovers it because the missing item is a label definition, not cells. KRT8-high or Cldn4-high cells must not be relabelled KAC to fill the gap. Two targeted requests would settle it: the author KAC classifier or annotated object, and the animal/treatment-history crosswalk for the later endpoint. |
| C273 | Three months of anti-IL-1beta leaves no gene differentially expressed at BH q<0.05 in any independently reviewed mouse compartment | trial U4 receiver differential expression, 4 Control IgG against 3 anti-IL-1beta animals, one retained library per animal, per-animal raw-count pseudobulks, TMM, voom and BH: 0 genes at q<0.05 in each direction in all seven receivers (alveolar broad 9,814 genes tested; fibroblast broad 11,625; fibroblast_Gsn 9,738; fibroblast_Itga8 7,126; fibroblast_Pi16 10,052; myeloid broad 9,667; CSF1R_myeloid 9,047). This made the declared conditional ligand-target arm, which needs ten mapped q<0.05 targets per direction, ineligible without relaxing the threshold Run record: `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u4_mouse_niche/validation.json`. | `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u4_mouse_niche/DE_target_eligibility.csv`; `Research Article/gate2_C3_yu_lee_choi_min_2026/trials/u4_mouse_niche/validation.json` | Not established (no discovery at the declared threshold; 4 against 3 animals bounds nothing; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Moderate, mostly as a power statement: an empty result across seven compartments at 4 against 3 animals is what this design can deliver, and it is not evidence that blockade did nothing, nor that neutralization succeeded. No equivalence follows. A discriminating experiment needs more animals per arm and a measured neutralization readout (mature IL-1beta protein or receptor occupancy) rather than more transcriptome depth. |

It sits with C161 and C163, where the same correlation question decided earlier IPF results. ## Question-level results: regulatory states and delivery (A1, A2, A8, A14, A15), registered 2026-09-29

A1, A2 and A15 results, plus the two endpoint requirements that A8 and A14 own.
A8 and A14 have computed nothing; each folder holds a README, rationale, plan and
contract only, and their rows (C339, C340) register what is missing rather than a
finding. C329, the one Validated row, is validated as a decision record: a frozen
depth rule refused a nominally significant correlation that proved to be depth.
The A2 screen's unit is the well, and four repeat wells are aliquots of one
cell-Matrigel mixture rather than independent preparations.

| # | Claim | Analyses behind it | Artefact | Status | Potential |

| C336 | Both declared alternative-normalisation sensitivities agreed with the standardised CPM primary in the rival-2 side branch | The original median-of-ratios sensitivity divided each count by both its size factor and its library total, double-removing depth; a regression test demonstrates the failure of that denominator on identical compositions where library B is exactly twice A. Its reported values (-0.1491 transitional, -0.0425 identity) are withdrawn as evidence. The replacement, specified and committed at f3950253ae229f062dbae94551b04a908a8e5ade before corrected outcomes were computed, estimates size factors from 15,174 reference genes positive in all eight epithelial libraries out of 42,548 matrix rows, excluding the seven scored panel genes and Itgb6, with no treatment-label or effect-based selection; factors range 0.7488 to 1.3262 and are centred to geometric mean 1. Corrected panels: transitional -0.085856 (p 0.685714, shift -0.048786 [-0.523072, +0.456674]) and identity +0.021399 (p 0.885714, shift +0.102947 [-1.005967, +0.683664]), both non-separating, so no sensitivity downgrade applies. Seven regression tests pass and an independent NumPy/SciPy verifier passes 62 of 62 checks Run record: `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/reports/NORMALIZATION_ERRATUM_2026-09-28.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2_normalization_correction_v1/correction_run.json`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2_normalization_correction_v1/independent_verification.json`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2_normalization_correction_v1/size_factors.tsv`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2_normalization_correction_v1/reference_genes.tsv` | Refuted (the original median-of-ratios sensitivity is invalid and withdrawn; the corrected replacement leaves the decision unchanged; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The separately computed CPM primary was not affected by the defect, and the corrected values are unstandardised log2 normalised counts rather than CPM, so the numerical differences between normalisation rows combine a repaired estimator, a broader reference and a changed score scale and must not be read as a common-unit effect change. The identity point estimate changes sign. The broad-reference estimator assumes stable or balanced reference abundance; without an absolute RNA reference, global abundance change remains unidentified. This repairs an analysis error and supplies no new biological units. |
| C337 | A public dataset exists that pairs an epithelial integrin perturbation with a measurement of activated TGF-beta | Recorded search before the question was written: 15 GEO DataSets queries through the Entrez gds index (hit counts 55, 9, 0, 4, 19, 6, 68, 2, 1, 3, 5, 4, 1447, 45, 82, with the inspected depth stated per query), plus PRIDE (3, 0 and 0 projects), the Image Data Resource and the BioImage Archive through BioStudies (1,622 hits, top 8 inspected). No deposit in any repository searched pairs an epithelial integrin perturbation with an activated-TGF-beta readout; the six nearest candidates are named with the specific reason each falls short, GSE190821 being nearest (replicated in vivo blockade, mouse as unit, but no activation readout, no separated fibroblast compartment and no ligand arm). Neither clinical programme against this integrin has deposited participant-level data Run record: `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/reports/PUBLIC_DATA_SEARCH.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/reports/PUBLIC_DATA_SEARCH.md`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/reports/REGISTER_DECISION.md`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/config/a15_question_contract.json` | Not establishable from public data at the recorded search (the binding gap is the readout, not the design; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | These are keyword searches over indexed metadata, so a deposit describing the same assay in different words could have been missed; this is a bounded search result, not proof of absence. Activated TGF-beta is distinguished from the latent pool by reporter bioassay, an active-dimer immunoassay or receptor-proximal signalling in the recipient, and those appear in figures rather than in sequence or image archives. The consequence carried into the question card is a named blocking constraint and a bench design, not vague insufficiency. The spatial layer is shared with A2 and must be counted once. |
| C338 | The A5/A11 injury residual module corroborates the epithelial null as a second, larger endpoint | Declared secondary on the same eight mice: 373 of 386 symbols measurable, mean difference -0.1412 at exact two-sided p 0.685714, non-separating. The Pearson correlation between its per-mouse score vector and that of the demoted A0 handling covariate is 0.8112, above the 0.7 at which the freeze required corroboration language to be dropped. Two sets containing the perturbed gene were barred from primary use: the A5/A11 shared remodelling module (11 of 12 members, contains Itgb6) was excluded outright and A1's eight-marker panel retained for per-gene reporting only Run record: `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2/stage3_execute_run.json`. | `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2/stage3_execute_run.json`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2/stage1_endpoint_coverage.tsv` | Refuted as corroboration (two overlapping sets from one source study scored on the same eight mice are nearly the same instrument; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Agreement between correlated instruments on the same animals adds no independent information, which is why the freeze set a correlation threshold in advance rather than after seeing agreement. This does not invalidate either score as a measurement; it removes their agreement as evidence. A genuinely independent secondary would need a differently sourced gene set or a different assay layer. |
| C339 | An eligible dataset linking frozen RNA maturation components to an independently measured mature AT1 endpoint in the same biological units exists in the surveyed evidence | Documentation pass recorded as outcome row O27 over the 29 inventoried outcome records. Relevant measured endpoint types already exist: O2, the AGER-positive fraction among Krt19-lineage-labelled cells in three named mice (32.84%, protein on labelled cells, independent of any RNA predictor panel), and O3, the published day-14 labelled-cell AGER endpoint under KIRA8, whose numerical source and unit count are not recovered in the folder. What is absent is a dataset in which compatible RNA components and a noncircular mature endpoint are measured in the same verified biological units; O2's three mice carry no early measurement, and O3's RNA and microscopy arms are different experiments in different animals. A8 has computed nothing of its own; its workspace holds a README, rationale, plan and question contract only Run record: `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/reports/A1_A8_A14_OUTCOME_INVENTORY.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/reports/A1_A8_A14_OUTCOME_INVENTORY.md`; `RQ_Specified/A8_maturation_component_at1_contribution/PLAN.md`; `RQ_Specified/A8_maturation_component_at1_contribution/config/a8_question_contract.json` | Not establishable at present (missing linkage and eligibility, not a missing endpoint category; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | This is a statement about the surveyed evidence, not proof that no eligible dataset exists; existing or author-provided data may qualify. The endpoint must be independently measured rather than a score-versus-score comparison, and the shared and disjoint components must be frozen before endpoint analysis. Concurrent measurement can serve the association route; the prospective-prediction route additionally requires earlier RNA. A8's founding observation is owned by another package and is a diagnostic of the definitions, not evidence for a maturation mechanism. Related rows: C168. |
| C340 | An eligible dataset measuring mature-cell yield, viability, traced descendants or function after verified IL-1beta withdrawal exists in the surveyed evidence | Documentation pass recorded as outcome row O28 over the same 29 inventoried outcome records: no eligible recovery dataset is identified, and A14 remains unexecuted with a README, rationale, plan and question contract only and no tables directory. The required contract is the relevant exposure or reception contrast linked to viable traced mature output, with independent animals or culture preparations whose identities are known and comparable post-withdrawal intervals against time-matched controls Run record: `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/reports/A1_A8_A14_OUTCOME_INVENTORY.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/reports/A1_A8_A14_OUTCOME_INVENTORY.md`; `RQ_Specified/A14_withdrawal_recovery_and_reception/PLAN.md`; `RQ_Specified/A14_withdrawal_recovery_and_reception/config/a14_question_contract.json` | Not establishable at present (no eligible recovery observations identified; this is not proof that new data generation is necessary; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | Loss of a transitional score after withdrawal cannot distinguish maturation, reversion, death or replacement, which is why viable traced mature output is part of the contract rather than an optional extra. A14's two hypotheses, exposure duration and compartment-specific reception, carry separate decisions and either can hold without the other; a design that changes both in an inseparable contrast answers neither. Its published figure asset is a proposed schematic with no result curves. |
| C341 | Three deposits used or screened by A1 carry internal contradictions between sample titles, file names and sample characteristics that remain unresolved | Metadata audit of 32 cached GEO catalog extracts with response URLs, retrieval times and SHA-256 hashes. GSE154965: GSM4685041 is titled Tigit_neg with characteristics tigit: Negative but all its supplementary files are named T_plus, and GSM4685042 is the mirror case. GSE273122: WT titles and filenames carry SP-C mutant characteristics and C121G titles carry SP-C wild-type characteristics; this uncertainty was deliberately not transferred into GSE273123, whose identities were separately resolved from SRA originals. GSE309751: GSM9278491 and GSM9278492 are titled 49 day mock PBS with mock/PBS peak filenames but carry treatment: Sendai virus infected. No label was swapped on the basis of expected expression Run record: `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/reports/catalog_audit.json`. | `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/metadata/README.md`; `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/metadata/GSE154965.json`; `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/metadata/GSE309751.json`; `RQ_Specified/A1_transitional_epithelial_state_distinction/metadata/GSE273122.json` | Descriptive only (verified metadata contradictions; the affected contrasts are held rather than corrected; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | These are observed inconsistencies in deposited metadata, not evidence about the underlying samples, and each requires an independent sample/file mapping or an author correction. The GSE309751 case is why the AP-1 bulk accessibility series is used as context rather than as a control-defined contrast. The correct handling is to hold the affected comparison; inferring the intended label from expression would make the analysis circular. |
| C342 | A between-arm-minus-within-arm mean distance statistic can be read as a change in the epithelium's overall profile | Simulation run in this repository before accepting a reviewer's claim, over 200 draws with eight mice and 200 genes. Under a pure between-mouse dispersion difference with no gene's mean moving, the between-minus-within statistic rejects in 1.000 of draws while the centroid-distance statistic rejects in 0.635, with a median within-arm distance ratio of 2.77; under the true null with equal dispersion both reject in 0.035; under a true location shift both reject in 1.000. Freeze v3 therefore makes the centroid distance primary and gates it on a within-arm distance ratio, which in the executed analysis is 1.0661 against the declared 1.5 threshold; the demoted statistic is reported as -0.038365 at p 0.6286 and carries no decision Run record: `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/reports/RIVAL2_FREEZE_V2_WITHDRAWN.md`. | `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/reports/RIVAL2_FREEZE_V2_WITHDRAWN.md`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/tables/rival2/stage3_execute_run.json`; `RQ_Specified/A15_epithelial_integrin_tgfb_activation/config/a15_rival2_freeze_v3.json` | Refuted (the statistic rejects on dispersion alone, so it is not a location test; delegated reassessment 2026-09-29) | The simulation is a property of the statistic, not of this deposit, so it generalises to any small-n omnibus of this form; it does not show that the executed centroid statistic is well powered, only that it is readable here because the dispersion gate passed. Nothing had been scored under the withdrawn freeze, so no result was revised. The lesson the freeze encodes is to declare the dispersion diagnostic before the statistic it guards. |

## Coverage of the register, 29 September 2026

Every folder under `Research Article/` and `RQ_Specified/` has now been
inventoried, and rows C169-C342 were added on 2026-09-29. The coverage ledgers
`analysis/claims/coverage_2026-09-29.csv` and
`analysis/claims/coverage_2026-09-29_second_pass.csv` list each document that was
read and the rows that register it, or the reason it holds no registrable claim.

Two exceptions are worth naming. A8 and A14 hold Stage 0 design material only, so
their rows record absent endpoint requirements rather than results. And the
Yu, Lee, Choi and Min package contributes two rows through A12 (C225 and C226)
that are not repeated in its own section.
